## 2. Data, model, sampling

| Symbol | Type | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| $i$ | index | unit, $i=1,\dots,n$ | plan §1 |
| $n$ | integer | sample size | plan §1 |
| $Y_{i1},Y_{i2}$ | scalar | outcome in periods 1 and 2 | plan §1 |
| $X_{i1},X_{i2}\in\mathcal X\subset\IR$ | scalar | continuous, potentially endogenous regressor | plan §1 |
| $Z_{i1},Z_{i2}\in\IR^p$ | vector | exogenous controls | plan §1 |
| $W_i\in\{0,1,\dots,\mathsf L-1\}$ | scalar | **discrete** instrument on $\mathsf L\ge2$ cells, $\mathsf L$ **fixed and known**; **cell $0$ is the base cell**. **RESERVED**: the realised value is written $0,1,\dots,\mathsf L-1$ or a bound index $l$, never $w$ ($w=\exp$ is the Bierens weight, C71's $\mathsf w$ the envelope value). **RETYPED 2026-08-10: this row printed the binary $W_i\in\{0,1\}$ and the gloss "binary instrument", which the corpus has not said since `eq:contrast`.** $W$ is *not* vector-valued — it is the scalar cell label; what is vector-valued is the contrast $\widetilde W$ built from it | `assumptions_main.tex:277–281`, `def:model` |
| $\eta_i$ | scalar | time-invariant individual effect | draft `:67` |
| $U_{i1},U_{i2}$ | scalar | idiosyncratic error | draft `:69` |
| $\Delta Y,\Delta Z,\Delta U$ | scalar / $\IR^p$ / scalar | first differences, period 2 minus period 1 | plan §1 |
| $p$ | integer | $\dim(Z)=\dim(\Delta Z)$. $\Delta Z$ includes a constant. **RESERVED** | plan §1 |
| $d_Z$ | integer | **effective** dimension of $\Delta Z$ for the propensity regression: the number of non-degenerate coordinates. $d_Z\le p-1$, because $\Delta Z$'s constant coordinate carries no information | this file (§6.3) |
| $\mathcal X$ | set | support of $X$; **compact, declared by A0** (not by C1, which only bounds densities on it). `lem:levels` cites A0 for exactly this | A0 |

## 3. Parameters, functions, and the difference structure

| Symbol | Type | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| $\vdagger=\varphi_\dagger$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR$ | true structural function, scalar argument | plan §1 |
| $\theta_\dagger\in\IR^p$ | vector | true linear coefficient | plan §1 |
| $\varphi$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR$ | generic / trial structural function | plan §1 |
| $\mathcal V$ | set | parameter space for $\varphi$: $\{\varphi\in L^2(\pi):\int\varphi\,d\pi=0\}$ | this file |
| $\Delta$ | operator $L^2(\pi)\to L^2(P)$ | period-difference: $\Delta\varphi:=\varphi(X_2)-\varphi(X_1)$ | plan §1 |
| $\Delta U(\theta,\varphi)$ | scalar r.v. | residual at trial values: $\Delta Y-\Delta\varphi-\Delta Z'\theta$. **Not** "$\Delta$ applied to $U(\theta,\varphi)$" | plan §1 |
| $h$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR$ | direction in $L^2(\pi)$; typically $h=\hat\varphi-\vdagger$ | memo |
| $\pi$ | density on $\mathcal X$ | ~~reference density: stationary density of $X$ if it exists, otherwise a convex combination of $f_{X_1},f_{X_2}$~~ — **RETYPED 2026-08-27 (C112). $\pi$ is the *pooled design density* $\tfrac12\big(f_{X_1}+f_{X_2}\big)$: a functional of $P$, unknown, estimated in implementation at $\hat\pi$ with floor $\varepsilon_\pi$, and *not* a reference measure the analyst picks.** The struck description was superseded by `def:spaces` **and by C109, written in this same file on 2026-08-26** — the authorial ruling that fixed the status — **and C109 did not chase its own ruling into this row**, so a binding register described the project's most-used density one way in §1.5l and another way in §3 for a full round. *That is the C71 species with the two records inside one file, and it is the reason §1.5g(iii) is owed a clause requiring a status ruling to sweep the type tables, not only the collision tables.* Two consequences the pooled form buys for free are at `rem:spacesread`(4b): $\bar c_\pi\le2$, and C1(ii)'s bounds on $\pi$ following from its bounds on $f_{X_1},f_{X_2}$ | `def:spaces`, C109, `rem:spacesread`(4) |
| $\nu_g$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR$ | **difference** functional $E[g\mid X_2=x]f_{X_2}(x)-E[g\mid X_1=x]f_{X_1}(x)$; $\nu:=\nu_{\Delta Z}\in\IR^p$ | memo §0.1 |
| $\sigma_g$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR$ | **sum** functional $E[g\mid X_2=x]f_{X_2}(x)+E[g\mid X_1=x]f_{X_1}(x)$ | memo §0.1 |
| $q$ | $\mathcal X^2\to\IR^p$ | $q(x_1,x_2)=E[\partial_u\ell_\star\,\widetilde W\mid X_1=x_1,X_2=x_2]$ — **the contrast stands to the RIGHT of the $p\times(\mathsf L-1)$ derivative**, which is the only order that multiplies and is the order $\psi$ carries at `eq:psidef`. **ORDER CORRECTED 2026-08-10: this row printed $E[\widetilde W\,\partial_u\ell_\star\mid\cdot]$, which is ill-typed at $\mathsf L\ge3$ and was undetectable at $\mathsf L=2$, where $\widetilde W$ is a scalar and the two orders coincide. The corpus states the order in words one line below each of its two displays.** **$\IR^p$-valued**, because the $p\times(\mathsf L-1)$ derivative contracted against $\widetilde W\in\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$ is (C18 as generalised). **Dispatch 13: $q$ is the object on the NON-vanishing side of the $\sigma$-field boundary and it is why the dispatch-10 error was plausible.** It conditions on $\sigma(X_1,X_2)$, where $P(W=l\mid\cdot)=p_{X,l}\ne\varrho_l$ ((P4b)'s warning), so $q\ne0$; $E[\ell_\star\widetilde W\Delta Z']$ conditions on $\sigma(\Delta U,\Delta Z)$, where A1 gives $P(W=l\mid\cdot)=\varrho_l$ ((P4c)), so it IS zero. **A reader who has $q$ in mind will read the second as non-vanishing too.** *(Both halves retyped 2026-08-10: the cell was fixed at $1$ and the second display carried the contrast on the left. `results_supp.tex:137` and `:5457` print $E[\ell_\star\widetilde W\Delta Z']$.)* | memo §3.5, C18, `results_supp.tex:1123`, `:1448–1450` |
| $\sigma_q$ | $\mathcal X\to\IR^p$ | the multiplier of `prp:sharpquad`; **replaces the memo's $M$** (C7). **$\IR^p$-valued** (C18), so `prp:sharpquad` is stated either coordinatewise or for a fixed direction $\vartheta'\psi$ with $\vartheta\in\IR^p$, $\|\vartheta\|=1$ — see §8.4 item 2. ~~stated for a fixed direction $\varsigma'\psi$, $\varsigma\in\IR^p$~~ **Round-7 correction, in place:** this row named $\varsigma$, which §8.4 item 2 and C25 forbid as a *binding* symbol — $\varsigma$ is a reserved **local** bound variable (§1.6) and the Step-4 direction is $\vartheta$. The row therefore contradicted, in the binding voice, the very section it pointed at. **$\psi$ is correct here** and is not changed: it is the moment function in its RESERVED sense, and $\vartheta'\psi$ is the scalar moment for the linear combination $\vartheta'\theta_\dagger$, which is C25's own mnemonic | this file, C18, C25 |
| $\mathcal D$ | operator | differentiation; $\mathcal D^{-1}$ integration, kernel $\un(x\ge v)$. Appears only in the levels/derivative remark | C2 |

## 4. The instrument channel and the debiasing weight

| Symbol | Type | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| $\mathsf L$ | integer $\ge2$ | **the number of instrument cells, fixed and known to the analyst.** *Sans-serif $\mathsf L$ is the cell count and nothing else, and it **never carries a subscript**; the one other sans-serif $L$ in the project, $\mathsf L_\Gamma$, **always** carries its subscript $\Gamma$ and is `lem:bierenscore`'s Laplace transform.* Italic $L$ remains the debiasing weight, $L_x,L_u$ the sieve dimensions, $L^2(\cdot)$ the function spaces — see the amended $L$ row of §1.7. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10: the symbol had governed every type in this corpus since the discrete generalisation and had no row here** | `assumptions_main.tex:277–281`, and the collision record at `:269–273` |
| $\varrho$ | $\IR^p\to\Delta_{\mathsf L-1}$, the simplex $\{v\in\IR^{\mathsf L-1}:v_l\ge0\ \forall l,\ \mathbf 1'v\le1\}$ | the **vector of non-base cell propensities** $\varrho=(\varrho_1,\dots,\varrho_{\mathsf L-1})'$, $\varrho_l(z)=P(W=l\mid\Delta Z=z)$. **RESERVED**; never $p(\cdot)$, never $\rho$. **RETYPED 2026-08-10: this row printed $\IR^p\to(0,1)$ and $E[W\mid\Delta Z]$, both of which are the $\mathsf L=2$ specialisation** — there $\varrho=\varrho_1=P(W=1\mid\Delta Z)$ and $\un\{W=1\}=W$ make $E[W\mid\Delta Z]$ the same number, which is why the scalar reading survived. At $\mathsf L\ge3$, $E[W\mid\Delta Z]$ is a mean of cell *labels* and is not a propensity at all | `eq:contrast`, `assumptions_main.tex:282–290`, `results_s3_estimation.tex:221` |
| $\varrho_l$, $\varrho_0$ | $\IR^p\to[0,1]$ | **cell probabilities.** $\varrho_l(z)=P(W=l\mid\Delta Z=z)$ for $l=1,\dots,\mathsf L-1$ are the coordinates of $\varrho$; the **base cell** probability is the residual $\varrho_0=1-\mathbf 1'\varrho$ and is *not* a coordinate of $\varrho$. **A4(i) quantifies over all $\mathsf L$ cells including cell $0$** — $\varrho_l\ge\varepsilon_\varrho$ for every $l\in\{0,\dots,\mathsf L-1\}$ — and the base cell is in that quantifier *for a reason and not for symmetry*: $\varrho_0\to0$ sends $\|\Omega^{-1}\|\to\infty$ however far the non-base cells stay from zero. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10** | `assumptions_main.tex:282–290`, A4(i), `assumptions_supp.tex:1029–1032` |
| $\mathbf 1$ | vector in $\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$ | **the vector of ones**, used only in $\varrho_0=1-\mathbf 1'\varrho$, in the simplex constraint $\mathbf 1'v\le1$, and in `eq:omegainv`'s $\mathbf 1\mathbf 1'$. **Bold roman, joining $\mathbf D$, $\mathbf B$ and the multi-index $\mathbf j$; it is not the indicator $\un\{\cdot\}$**, which is `\un` throughout. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10** | `assumptions_main.tex:290`, `assumptions_supp.tex:1008–1019` |
| $\Omega$ | $\IR^p\to\IR^{(\mathsf L-1)\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | the **conditional contrast covariance** $\Omega(z):=\operatorname{Var}(\widetilde W\mid\Delta Z=z)=\operatorname{diag}(\varrho(z))-\varrho(z)\varrho(z)'$, symmetric positive semidefinite. It enters as a covariance and as the prefactor `eq:omegaprefac`, $E[\widetilde W\un\{\Delta U\le u\}\mid\Delta Z=z]=\Omega(z)d(u\mid z)$, **and never as a weight**. Under A4(i), $\lambda_{\min}(\Omega)\ge\varepsilon_\varrho/\mathsf L$ (`eq:omegafloor`, spectral norm). At $\mathsf L=2$, $\Omega=\varrho(1-\varrho)$ is the scalar prefactor every earlier round carried. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10, and it is now the ONLY object the glyph $\Omega$ names anywhere in this corpus** — the intertwining family that shared it is $\mathcal O$ since 2026-08-09, so **no disambiguation rule attaches to $\Omega$**; §1.7's three rows that still printed one were retyped in this pass | `eq:omegadef`, `eq:omegaprefac`, `eq:omegafloor`, `results_supp.tex:3095–3100` |
| $c_{\widetilde W}$ | constant $\in\{1,\sqrt2\}$ | $c_{\widetilde W}:=\min\{\sqrt2,\sqrt{\mathsf L-1}\}$, the **surely-valid Euclidean bound** $\|\widetilde W\|\le c_{\widetilde W}$ of (P1b). **It equals $1$ if and only if $\mathsf L=2$, and $\sqrt2$ at every $\mathsf L\ge3$ — so it saturates at $\mathsf L=3$ and does not grow with the number of cells.** *Italic and symbol-subscripted, joining $c_\Phi,c_\Pi,c_C,c_\mu,c_{\mu''}$; every sans-serif $\mathsf c_j$ is integer-subscripted, so the font separates the families without a further rule.* **It is not delivered by (P1)**, which is the per-cell bound $\lvert\widetilde W_l\rvert\le1$: the Euclidean bound on the vector is a different statement and is not $1$. It is the constant every contraction of $\widetilde W$ against a vector carries — $\|\Upsilon\|$, $\|\mathcal B_{\mathrm{mult}}\|$, `lem:secondord`'s sup-norms. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10** | `results_supp.tex:946`, `:953–973`, (P1b) at `proofs.tex:871–873` |
| $\widetilde W^{\Omega}$ | r.v. in $\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$ | the **normalised (inverse-probability) contrast** $\Omega(\Delta Z)^{-1}\widetilde W$, with $\widetilde W^\Omega_l=\un\{W=l\}/\varrho_l-\un\{W=0\}/\varrho_0$: the Horvitz–Thompson contrast of each cell against the base. **INTERPRETATION ONLY. The estimator carries the RAW contrast `eq:contrast` on both sides; nothing in the assumption block, in $\psi$ or in the operator is stated at $\widetilde W^\Omega$**, and the paper does not estimate at it. Defined and confined to `rem:htread`. **NEW ROW 2026-08-10, and it is here so that a reader who meets $\Omega^{-1}$ once does not infer a weighting scheme the corpus declines** | `eq:omeganorm`, `rem:htread`, `assumptions_main.tex:332–338` |
| $\widetilde W$ | r.v. in $\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$ | the **contrast vector** $\widetilde W=(\widetilde W_1,\dots,\widetilde W_{\mathsf L-1})'$, $\widetilde W_l=\un\{W=l\}-\varrho_l(\Delta Z)$; conditionally centred **at $\sigma(\Delta Z)$ by construction ((P3)) and at $\sigma(\Delta U,\Delta Z)$ under A1 and only under A1 ((P4c)) — and at NEITHER larger $\sigma$-field, in particular not at $\sigma(X_1,X_2,\Delta Z)$, where $p_{X,l}\ne\varrho_l$**. (P3) is an identity in $\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$. That two-line scope statement is the whole of dispatch 13's finding and it is stated here because this row is where a reader acquires the word "centred". **THE BOUNDS ARE TWO SEPARATE STATEMENTS AND THIS ROW PRINTED ONLY THE SCALAR ONE UNTIL 2026-08-10.** *Per-cell* **(P1)**: $\lvert\widetilde W_l\rvert\le1$ free, at every $l$, from $\un\{W=l\}\in\{0,1\}$ and $\varrho_l\in[0,1]$, sharpening at *every* cell to $\le1-\varepsilon_\varrho<1$ **only under A4(i)** — the strict bound is false without overlap, $\varrho_l=0$ with $W=l$ giving $\widetilde W_l=1$. *Euclidean, on the vector* **(P1b)**: $\|\widetilde W\|\le c_{\widetilde W}=\min\{\sqrt2,\sqrt{\mathsf L-1}\}$, **which is not $1$ at $\mathsf L\ge3$ and is not a consequence of (P1)**. What the operator norms, the kernel bounds and the Hilbert–Schmidt constant consume is (P1); what a contraction of $\widetilde W$ against a vector consumes is (P1b); *a derivation routed through the wrong one does not reproduce the printed constants in either direction.* ~~$\lvert\widetilde W\rvert\le1$ **free** (from $W\in\{0,1\}$, $\varrho\in[0,1]$)~~ — **struck: an unscoped scalar modulus on a vector-valued object, and it is the species Phase 5f closed corpus-wide.** **Sweep, d-glossary-sync, over the eight compiled files and this one, patterns `\lvert\widetilde W`, `\|\widetilde W`, `\big|\widetilde W` and the same four on `\widehat{\widetilde W}`: the compiled corpus's surviving scalar moduli of a contrast are `proofs.tex:889` (on $\widetilde W$) and `:8971` (on $\widehat{\widetilde W}$ and on $\mathsf r$), and every one of them is inside a sentence that opens "At $\mathsf L=2$".** The unscoped one struck here was the only unscoped hit in that set. *The retired mirrors carry four more — `results.tex:1811`, `:4958`, `assumptions.tex:733`, `results_main.tex:457` — and they are **not** repaired, because no document compiles them; they are named so that a reader who greps the whole directory is not surprised.* This row sat in a required deliverable rather than in a compiled file, which is why Phase 5f's corpus-wide sweep did not reach it — *the same reason C108 gives for its own item, one generalisation later.* **$\widehat{\widetilde W}^{(-k)}$ is centred at NO $\sigma$-field**, which is why `eq:Jhat`'s second summand does not vanish | `eq:contrast`, (P1)/(P1b)/(P3) at `proofs.tex:866–890`, F-1 |
| $\Phi$ | $\IR^p\to\IR^p$ | bounded one-to-one transform of $\Delta Z$; componentwise $\arctan$, $c_\Phi:=\sup_z\|\Phi(z)\|_\infty=\pi/2$ | plan §1 |
| $c_\Phi$ | constant | $=\pi/2$; **value never read by a proof**, only finiteness | this file |
| $w$ | $\IR\to\IR$ | **$w=\exp$, and only $\exp$** (B1(ii)). A general real-analytic non-polynomial weight is **inadmissible** against a bounded odd $\Phi$ — see C-1 and `rem:swintercept`; the intercept-indexed Stinchcombe–White family is the correct route to generality and is not adopted. **RESERVED as a function symbol**: $w$ is never a realised value of $W$ | B1(ii), C-1 |
| $\bar w(t)$ | function | $e^{c_\Phi\|t\|_1}$; the envelope of $\lvert w(t'\Phi(\cdot))\rvert$ | B1(ii) |
| $t\in\mathcal T$ | vector | Bierens index; $\mathcal T\subset\IR^p$ of positive Lebesgue measure | plan §1 |
| ~~$\mathcal T_n$~~ | ~~set~~ | ~~finite growing index family used on the $\varphi$-side, $\#\mathcal T_n\to\infty$. **Replaces the memo's $\mathcal T_{K_n}$** ($K$ is the fold count)~~ — **ROW RETIRED 2026-08-27 (C112), on two independent grounds.** *(1) The object is not instantiated.* `assumptions_supp.tex:500–517` carries B1(vi) **struck** and says in compiled text that the growing finite family "is instantiated **nowhere in this project**"; the only compiled occurrences of the symbol are inside that retirement record, i.e. **mentions and not uses**. *This is the `\mathsf H` discriminant applied and answered the other way*: the concept did not survive under a new glyph, and it did not leave with a proof either — **it was never instantiated**, so the row reserves nothing and misleads. *(2) The parenthesis is false*: "$K$ is the fold count" — the fold count is $\mathsf F$, `def:crossfit`, and italic $K$ is `lem:pconv`'s tolerance level. **One row, one retired object and one wrong letter** | ~~this file~~ retired |
| $\gamma$ | finite signed measure on $\mathcal T$ | B1(v)'s **correction measure**, with $\int_{\mathcal T}\bar w\,d\lvert\gamma\rvert<\infty$ and, where $\partial_uL$ is required, $\lvert\partial_u\lambda(u,t)\rvert\le\Lambda_1(t)$ with $\int\Lambda_1\bar w\,d\lvert\gamma\rvert<\infty$. **Was $\mu$ in plan §1** (C5). **RESIDUAL RECORDED AND NOT REPAIRED, 2026-08-10.** This row printed the construction ~~$L(u,z)=\int_{\mathcal T}\lambda(u,t)\,w(t'\Phi(z))\,\gamma(dt)$~~ as though it were B1(v)'s content. It is not: **B1(v) as compiled states only the two integrability clauses and prints no such display**, and the display is in any case **type-inconsistent now that $L$ is $\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$-valued** while $\lambda$, $w$ and $\gamma$ are all scalar — it would produce a scalar $L$. *Resolving it needs a decision about which of $\lambda$ or $\gamma$ carries the two indices, and that decision belongs to whoever next opens B1(v), not to a glossary sweep.* The clauses above are quoted from the compiled assumption and are safe to rely on; **the struck display is not** | B1(v) at `assumptions_main.tex:977–980` |
| $\lambda$ | $\IR\times\mathcal T\to\IR$ | weight in the residual direction | plan §1 |
| $L$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | debiasing weight on $(u,z)$. **$\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$-valued: one ROW per coordinate of $\theta$ (C18) and one COLUMN per contrast.** *Norms carried by $L$ and by everything built from it are **Frobenius**; matrix norms elsewhere in this corpus are spectral unless the site says otherwise.* **RETYPED 2026-08-10 from $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^p$**, which is the $\mathsf L=2$ specialisation, a $p\times1$ matrix being a vector in $\IR^p$ and Frobenius being Euclidean. **N1 is a *property* a weight may or may not have, not a normalisation built into $L$** — the round-6 gloss "normalised by N1" is withdrawn, because under it `prp:orthog`(iii)'s equivalence has no content (see the round-7 note under N1 in `assumptions.tex`) | plan §1, C18 |
| $L^{\,b}_{\ell}$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | the weight **generated by $\ell$ and anchored at $b$**: $L^{\,b}_\ell(u,z)=\int_0^u\ell(v,z)dv+b(z)$, **the integral taken entrywise** and well defined because each entry of $\ell(\cdot,z)$ is continuous and bounded. The unique $u$-antiderivative of $\ell$ with $L^{\,b}_\ell(0,\cdot)=b$, for a Borel $b:\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$. *Admissible* if $E\|b(\Delta Z)\|^2<\infty$, **the norm Frobenius**. **RETYPED 2026-08-10** (codomain, and the norm named) | `def:weight`(b), `eq:weightdef` |
| $L_\star$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | $:=L^{\,b_\star}_{\ell_\star}$, the weight attached to the selected representer at the anchor fixed by A2′(iii). **RETYPED 2026-08-10.** **This is the object $\psi$, `lem:secondord`, `prp:orthog`(ii)–(iii), A5(iv) and the variance are about.** It was used at all of those sites and defined nowhere before round 7 | `def:weight`, A2′(iii) |
| $b_\star$ | $\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | the **anchor** $L_\star(0,\cdot)$. Free of the adjoint equation, priced by A2′(iii) at the Frobenius norm. Zero anchor and N1 anchor are both admissible with no further assumption (`rem:anchor`). **RETYPED 2026-08-10, and the corpus had already recorded this as an open contradiction rather than a drift**: `def:weight`(b) printed $b:\IR^p\to\IR^p$ while A2′(iii) printed $b_\star:\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$, and Phase 2a closed it in A2′(iii)'s direction — the closing paragraph of `def:weight` sets $L_\star:=L^{\,b_\star}_{\ell_\star}$, so the two must agree by construction. **The DOMAIN $\IR^p$ is $\Delta Z$'s space and is untouched** | A2′(iii), `rem:anchor`, `results_s2_identification.tex:1915`, `:1957–1965` |
| $\ell$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | $\partial_u L$, taken entrywise; equivalently $\ell=\mathcal W\ell^\Pi$, **columnwise in $l$** — column $l$ is generated by the slice $\ell^\Pi(\cdot,\cdot,l)$ and by no other, so the $\mathsf L-1$ contrasts carry $\mathsf L-1$ *separately choosable* generators (separately choosable, **not** linearly independent: nothing prevents two slices coinciding). **RETYPED 2026-08-10 from $\IR^p$-valued (C18), which is the $\mathsf L=2$ reading** | `def:weight`, `results_s2_identification.tex:1883–1891`, C18 |
| $\ell^\Pi_\dagger$ | element of $L^2(\Pi;\IR^p)$, i.e. a function of $(u,t,l)$ with values in $\IR^p$ | minimum-$L^2(\Pi)$-norm solution of $\mathcal A^\ast_{\mathrm{cond}}\ell^\Pi=-\nu/\pi$. **The right-hand side is $-\nu/\pi$, not $-\nu$** (M-1/F-12). **Renamed from $\ell_\dagger$ in dispatch 2**: the superscript $\Pi$ marks the range-space object and separates it from its observable-space image. **RETYPED 2026-08-10: the space is $L^2(\Pi;\IR^p)$ of `def:spaces` and the third argument is the contrast index** | C4, C17, F-12, `def:spaces` |
| $\ell^\Pi_\star$ | as $\ell^\Pi_\dagger$ | the selected solution in $\ell^\Pi_\dagger+\ker\mathcal A^\ast_{\mathrm{cond}}$; **a function of $(u,t,l)$** — $t$ the Bierens index, $l\in\{1,\dots,\mathsf L-1\}$ the contrast index, which is the third factor of $\Pi$. Under A2′ it is an *admissible selection* in the sense of `def:weight`; its slices lie in $H^{\mu''}(\IR;\IR^p)$ (A2c). **RETYPED 2026-08-10: this row printed "a function of $(u,t)$", which the type declaration binding on every statement in the corpus contradicts** | C4, C17, `assumptions_main.tex:320–322`, `def:spaces` |
| $\ell_\dagger$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | $:=\mathcal W\ell^\Pi_\dagger$, the observable-space image of the minimum-norm solution. **RETYPED 2026-08-10, and this row was NOT on the sweep list that produced the rest of this pass — it is the same species, found by asking which other rows are $\mathcal W$-images.** **Bare, and correctly so**: §8.5's rule is that the *range-space* objects carry the superscript $\Pi$, not that $\ell_\dagger$ is forbidden. The row was missing through round 6 | `lem:solset`(i) |
| $\ell_\star$ | $\IR\times\IR^p\to\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$ | $:=\mathcal W\ell^\Pi_\star$, the observable-space representer, **columnwise in $l$**. **Constructed, not assumed** (C17, N-10 closed): `lem:adjoint`(ii) makes $\mathcal W$ bounded into $L^2(P_{\Delta U,\Delta Z};\IR^{\mathsf L-1})$ in each of the $p$ coordinates of $\theta$, so $\ell_\star(\Delta U,\Delta Z)$ is automatically a well-defined square-integrable random **matrix**. **RETYPED 2026-08-10.** **This is the object A2′(i)–(ii) and `eq:JSig` speak about** (through dispatch 12 this row said "and A3"; A3 is retired). **It is a Borel map of $(u,z)$ and of nothing else — in particular not of $W$ — and that is what `lem:momzero`(iv) consumes: the SELECTION of $\ell^\Pi_\star$ from $\ell^\Pi_\dagger+\ker\mathcal A^\ast_{\rm cond}$ may depend on the joint law including the law of $W$, which does not make $\ell_\star(\Delta U,\Delta Z)$ anything other than $\sigma(\Delta U,\Delta Z)$-measurable** | C17, `lem:adjoint` |
| $\mathcal W$ | $L^2(\Pi)\to L^2(P_{\Delta U,\Delta Z};\IR^{\mathsf L-1})$, applied **coordinatewise** in $\theta$ and **columnwise** in $l$ | Bierens induction map, **diagonal in the contrast index**: $(\mathcal W\ell)_l(u,z)=\varpi_{\mathsf w}(l)\,\varpi_u(u)\int_{\mathcal T}\ell(u,t,l)\,w(t'\Phi(z))\,\Pi_t(dt)$ for $l=1,\dots,\mathsf L-1$. Bounded, range dense **in the $\IR^{\mathsf L-1}$-valued space and not merely componentwise**, $\ker\mathcal W\subseteq\ker\mathcal A^\ast_{\mathrm{cond}}$. **RESERVED**: not the instrument $W$, not the weight $w$ — calligraphic only. Stated for a scalar coordinate of $\theta$ and applied to each of the $p$ coordinates of $\ell^\Pi$, which is how $\ell_\star=\mathcal W\ell^\Pi_\star$ is $\IR^{p\times(\mathsf L-1)}$-valued. **RETYPED 2026-08-10, and the mass $\varpi_{\mathsf w}(l)$ in column $l$ is load-bearing, not decorative**: it is repair R1 of 2026-08-09, and without it the passage from the slicewise estimate to the vector norm would cost $(\min_l\varpi_{\mathsf w}(l))^{-1/2}\ge1$ instead of $(\max_l\varpi_{\mathsf w}(l))^{1/2}\le1$, so the printed operator-norm constant would be too small | `eq:Wdef` at `results_supp.tex:768–791`, `lem:adjoint`(ii), §8.4 |
| $\psi$ | $\IR^p$-valued | the moment function $\psi(\theta,\zeta)=\Delta Z\,\Delta U(\theta,\varphi)+L\big(\Delta U(\theta,\varphi),\Delta Z\big)\,\widetilde W(\varrho)$, and at the truth $\psi_\star=\Delta Z\,\Delta U+L_\star(\Delta U,\Delta Z)\,\widetilde W$. **THE CONTRAST STANDS TO THE RIGHT OF THE $p\times(\mathsf L-1)$ WEIGHT. That is the only order that multiplies, and $\psi$ is $\IR^p$-valued because of it.** **ORDER CORRECTED 2026-08-10: this row printed $\widetilde W_iL(\cdots)$ — contrast on the LEFT — which is undefined at $\mathsf L\ge3$ and was invisible at $\mathsf L=2$, where $\widetilde W$ is a scalar and $L\widetilde W=\widetilde WL$.** The corpus states the order four times: `assumptions_main.tex:325`, `results_supp.tex:686`, `proofs.tex:1668–1670`, `:6246–6247`. **RESERVED**: the draft's $\psi$ in the adjoint display `:172–177` is renamed $\ell$, and `lem:bierenscore`'s Laplace transform is $\mathsf L_\Gamma$ (C26) | `eq:psidef`/`eq:psistar` at `results_s3_estimation.tex:226–234`, `assumptions_main.tex:324–330` |
| $\Psi(\tau)$ | $\IR^p$-valued | $E[\psi(\theta_\dagger,\zeta_\tau)]$, the moment along the nuisance path; finite on $[-\bar\tau,\bar\tau]\times\IR^2$. **Replaces the round-6 $m(\tau)$**, which collided with the LF iteration counter for the second time on that letter (C21) | `lem:secondord`, `eq:Psitau` |
| $\Psi_L(\tau_1)$ | $\IR^p$-valued | $E[\psi(\theta_\dagger,(\vdagger+\tau_1h,L,\varrho))]$, the moment along the **structural** direction only, at an arbitrary admissible weight $L$. **Finite on all of $\IR$, not merely on $[-\bar\tau,\bar\tau]$**, and defined without $\bar\tau$, without a direction pair $(D,\mathsf r)$ and without A2′ — which is what makes `prp:orthog`(i)'s hypothesis reduction readable off its own statement. $\Psi_{L_\star}(\tau_1)=\Psi(\tau_1,0,0)$. **Round-7 postfix: defined in place at `eq:PsiL`; it was previously imported from `lem:secondord`, whose hypothesis list contains A2′** | `prp:orthog`(i), `eq:PsiL` |
| $\zeta_\tau$, $\zeta_\star$ | triple | the nuisance triple $(\varphi,L,\varrho)$ at the perturbation $\tau$ and at the truth, $\zeta_\star=(\vdagger,L_\star,\varrho)$. **Always subscripted.** Bare $\zeta$ is a *generic function* — an element of $\mathcal V$, the inner function of (P7a), the argument of `eq:jackson` — which is what every bare $\zeta$ in the three files actually is (C28) | C8, C28 |

## 5. Operators, spaces, and regularisation

| Symbol | Type | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| $T(\varphi,\theta)$ | $\mathcal V\times\IR^p\to L^2(\Pi)$ | the operator of the model: $T(\varphi,\theta)=\big[(u,t,l)\mapsto\mathcal M(u,t,l;\varphi,\theta)\big]$, and the operator equation is $T(\varphi,\theta)=0$ in $L^2(\Pi)$. **RETYPED 2026-08-10, AND THE WORD THAT WAS WRONG IS "COORDINATES".** The row read "coordinates $\mathcal M(u,t;\varphi,\theta)$" — two range arguments, and a noun that says the contrast index is a **coordinate of the value**. It is not: it is an **argument**, so $T(\varphi,\theta)$ is a *scalar* function on the three-factor base set and the range space is `def:spaces`'s scalar $L^2(\Pi)$, not $L^2(\Pi;\IR^{\mathsf L-1})$. *The source column also pointed at `results.tex`, a retired mirror that no document compiles* | `eq:Tdef`/`eq:opeq` at `results_s2_identification.tex:221–239` (`def:operator`) |
| $\mathcal M(u,t,l;\varphi,\theta)$ | scalar | the Bierens moment family $E\big[\widetilde W_l\,\un\{\Delta U(\theta,\varphi)\le u\}\,w(t'\Phi(\Delta Z))\big]$, for $(u,t,l)\in\IR\times\mathcal T\times\{1,\dots,\mathsf L-1\}$; abbreviated $\mathcal M(u,t,l)$ at the truth. **RETYPED 2026-08-10: two range arguments and an unindexed contrast, where the corpus carries three and $\widetilde W_l$.** *The contrast index is an **argument of $\mathcal M$ and not a coordinate of its value**, which is what keeps $T(\varphi,\theta)$ a **scalar** function on the base set of $\Pi$ — the single most easily mis-read consequence of the generalisation, and the reason `def:spaces` says the range is the scalar space* | `eq:Mdef` at `results_s2_identification.tex:221–236` |
| $\hat T$ | random operator $\mathcal V\times\IR^p\to L^2(\Pi)$ | ~~sample analogue of $T$~~ **$\hat T(\varphi,\theta)(u,t,l)=n_{\rm m}^{-1}\sum_{i\in\mathcal I_m}\widehat{\widetilde W}_{i,l}\,\mathsf C_{h_u}(u-\Delta U_i(\theta,\varphi))\,w(t'\Phi(\Delta Z_i))$**, `eq:hatT`, for $(u,t,l)\in\IR\times\mathcal T\times\{1,\dots,\mathsf L-1\}$ — **the third argument and the cell subscript on $\widehat{\widetilde W}$ were both missing from this row until 2026-08-10**, so it printed a scalar-contrast operator on a two-factor range. **The struck entry was the whole row through round 9, and it pointed at memo prose**: $\hat T$ appeared in the *maintained* A6 and A7 with no defining display in any file, so those assumptions' content was undetermined. Now defined in `assumptions.tex` Part V, beside A7, under `def:hatT`'s two conventions: the fold split (A4(iii)) and truncation of $\hat\varrho$ to $[\varepsilon_\varrho,1-\varepsilon_\varrho]$ (A4(i)), the latter giving $\varepsilon_\varrho\le\lvert\widehat{\widetilde W}_{i,l}\rvert\le1$ **surely, per cell** (`proofs.tex:7357`; the Euclidean bound on the vector is $c_{\widetilde W}$ and is a different statement). **Depends on $\varphi$ through point evaluations $\varphi(X_{ij})$**, which is why it is nowhere Fréchet differentiable | `def:hatT`, `eq:hatT` |
| $\hat T_{h_x}$ | random operator | `eq:hatT` with $\Delta\varphi_i$ replaced by the smoothed difference $\Delta_{h_x}\varphi_i$; **depends on $\varphi$ through bounded linear functionals**, and is the operator $\hat{\mathcal A}_\varphi$ exactly differentiates (`prp:hatAprop`(iv)) and the one `def:lfstep` iterates on. The difference from $\hat T$ decides `prp:tccfails` (C52) | `eq:hatTx` |
| $\mathsf C_{h_u}(v;I)$, $\mathsf C_{h_x}$ | $\IR\to\IR$ | the **integrated** kernel $h^{-1}\int_{-\infty}^{v}C_h(\varsigma,I)d\varsigma$; $\mathsf C_{h_u}(v):=\mathsf C_{h_u}(v;\IR)$. The boundary index is carried in the $x$-direction only, $\mathcal X$ being a compact interval and $u$ ranging over $\IR$ — *in $u$ the interval is always $\IR$*, which the interval indexing says more directly than the scalar did. Sans-serif against roman $C_h$ (C49). **Re-indexed from $s_0$ to $I$ 2026-08-15** | `def:intker`, `lem:intker` |
| $\Delta_{h_x}\varphi_i$ | scalar, linear in $\varphi$ | the **smoothed period difference** $\int\mathsf d_i\varphi\,dx$ of `eq:smdiff`; the functional through which $\hat{\mathcal A}_\varphi$ and $\hat T_{h_x}$ see $\varphi$ (C50) | `eq:smdiff` |
| $\mathrm d\hat T_\varphi[v]$ | linear, **unbounded** | the Gateaux derivative of $\hat T(\cdot,\theta)$, `eq:gateaux`; exists in every direction and is unbounded on $\mathcal V$, so $\hat T$ is nowhere Fréchet differentiable. **Not an operator**, which is the point of naming it (C53) | `prp:hatAprop`(v) |
| $\mathbf G_n(\varphi)$ | $n_{\rm m}\times n_{\rm m}$ PSD | the Gram matrix of $\{\hat g_{\cdot,\cdot,i}(\varphi,\theta)\}$ in $L^2(\Pi)$, `eq:gram`. **Carries its base point**, because `prp:tccrepair`(ii) needs $\lambda_{\min}$ bounded below uniformly over base points (C54). Has a zero row iff some $\widehat{\widetilde W}_i=0$, which `def:hatT`'s truncation excludes surely | `eq:gram` |
| $\mathcal V(\varphi_\circ,\rho)$ | closed subset of $\mathcal V$ | the $L^2(\pi)$-ball of centre $\varphi_\circ$ and radius $\rho$, `eq:ball`. **Always written with both arguments**; bare $\mathcal V_n$ is the source-condition ball and is a different object (C63) | `eq:ball`, `def:a6b` |
| $n_{\rm m}$, $n_{\rm a}$ | integers | $\#\mathcal I_m$, $\#\mathcal I_a$; upright subscripts, against the italic LF counter $m$ (C51) | `lem:balance`, `def:hatT` |
| $\gamma_n$ | rate | the operator-estimation rate of `eq:gamman`: $\sup_{\varphi\in\mathcal V_n\cap\mathcal S_n}\|\hat{\mathcal A}_\varphi-\mathcal A_\varphi\|=O_P(\gamma_n)$. **Not consumed by `prp:phirate`**, which runs conditionally on the sample and imposes (P-a.3) on the compression of the realised operator; retained because it is the re-derivation C5′(ii) asks for (C56) | `lem:aerr` |
| $\alpha$ | number in $(0,\tfrac12)$ | **BINDING**: the propensity rate exponent, $\alpha:=r/(2r+d_Z)$, so that $\hat\delta_n=n^{-\alpha}$ **for all $n$ large enough** — A4(ii)'s $d_Z\ge1$ gives $\alpha<\tfrac12$ strictly, hence the propensity branch attains A5(i)'s maximum above the crossover $n^{1-2\alpha}=\ln n$ and not below it. **Repaired 2026-08-04**: this row read "in every configuration of interest", quoting A5(i)'s closing sentence, which was struck for being unquantified and false at finite $n$ over an admissible range. Appears in A4(ii)–(iii), A5(iii), C5′(i)'s amended bandwidth $h_u\asymp\hat\delta_n^{1/p_K}=n^{-\alpha/8}$, (P-a.2), (P-f), `eq:frontier`, `eq:lxwindow`, `eq:prelimrate`, `eq:muthreshold` and every row of the smoothness-budget table. **Withdrawn from §1.6's local list in round 12** (C72), where it had been declared local while binding at ~40 sites; the multi-index use is written out as $\mathbf j$, the generic $L^2(\Pi)$ element is $\Xi$, and the elementary-inequality use is $\varsigma,\varsigma'$ | A4(ii), C5′(i), C72 |
| $\mathsf R$ | constant $<\infty$ | the radius of the **$H^s$ ball** in `eq:srcball`, chosen large enough to bound $\|\vdagger\|_{H^s}$ as well. Sans-serif against the integer $R_n$ (C57). **Shared by seven places and therefore not a free tuning constant**: `def:srcball`, A5(vi′), (P-a.1), (P-c), (P-g), `eq:entropy` and `lem:unifop` Step 4, so the hypothesis is an existential over *one* radius meeting all seven demands. **Not the source radius (F-f) is applied at**, which is $\mathsf R_\ast$ | `def:srcball` |
| $\mathsf R_\ast$ | constant $<\infty$ | the **spectral pre-image norm** bound of (P-a.3): $\bar v^{(k)}_\dagger=(\hat{\mathcal P}^{\,\mathcal S})^{\mu}\varsigma^{(k)}$ with $\|\varsigma^{(k)}\|_{L^2(\pi)}\le\mathsf R_\ast$, w.p.$\to1$ and at every stage. Enters **only** through (F-f): `eq:linlf`, $\mathsf c_{17}=2c_\mu\mathsf R_\ast^{1/(2\mu+1)}$, hence $\mathsf c_{15}$, and (L4)'s stopping-index bound. **Distinct from $\mathsf R$ and not comparable to it in either direction** (C69), the only device that would relate them being the operator interpolation `rem:pasrc`(1) shows unavailable at $2\mu>1$; through round 11 one symbol carried both, on the two sides of an inequality `def:lfstep` must establish | (P-a.3), `rem:pasrc`, C69 |
| $\hat{\mathcal P}^{\,\mathcal S}_{\varphi}$ | operator $\mathcal S_n\to\mathcal S_n$ | the **compression** $P_{\mathcal S_n}\hat{\mathcal A}^\ast_\varphi\hat{\mathcal A}_\varphi\vert_{\mathcal S_n}$ of `eq:compress`, self-adjoint and positive; the operator (P-a.3) states the source condition at and the operator `def:lfstep`(b) actually regularises, since $\mathcal L^\ast\mathcal L=\omega_n^{-2}\hat{\mathcal P}^{\,\mathcal S}_{\varphi_\circ}$ by `eq:LstarL`. **Not the restriction $\hat{\mathcal A}^\ast\hat{\mathcal A}\vert_{\mathcal S_n}$**, whose range is nested with the compression's in neither direction; (L2) establishes the compression and rounds 1–11 had (L5) name the restriction | `eq:compress`, `eq:LstarL` |
| $\mathcal L$ | **bounded** linear operator into $L^2(\Pi)$ | **the operator (F-f) is applied to.** Generic in `lem:scaleinv` and `rem:ff`; instantiated in `prp:phirate` Step 2 and `lem:pathcont` at the one value $\omega_n^{-1}\hat{\mathcal A}_{\varphi_\circ}\vert_{\mathcal S_n}$, with $\mathcal L_{\omega_\ast}:=\omega_\ast^{-1}\mathcal L$ and $\|\mathcal L\|\le1$ **surely** by `eq:normconst`. **Boundedness is part of the typing, at every occurrence** — which is why `prp:tccfails`'s quantified candidate, instantiated at an *unbounded* map in its clause (iii), is $\mathcal Q$ and not $\mathcal L$ (C70). **This row was missing through round 11**, while the letter carried three readings | `rem:ff`, `lem:scaleinv`, `def:lfstep`, C70 |
| $\mathcal Q$; $\mathcal Q_1,\mathcal Q_2$ | operators, **no properties by default** | a **candidate** operator in a quantified statement: bare $\mathcal Q$ ranges over candidate linearisations of $\hat T$ in `prp:tccfails`(i)–(iii), including the unbounded Gateaux map at (iii); integer-subscripted $\mathcal Q_1,\mathcal Q_2$ are the generic positive operators of `lem:srctransfer`(ii.a)'s Löwner witness. **Calligraphic, against italic $Q_1,Q_2,Q_{12}$** (the regular conditional distributions of (P6b)) and blackboard $\IQ$ (the rationals) (C70) | `prp:tccfails`, `lem:srctransfer`(ii) |

| $\Theta_I$ | $\IR\to\IR$ | the kernel read as a function of its **scaled** argument, $\Theta_I(\varsigma):=C_h(h\varsigma,I)$, `eq:rescaled`. **$\Theta_1:=\Theta_{\IR}$ is the interior member and the subscript $1$ is an ABBREVIATION for $I=\IR$. It is the DIGIT that is not an interval and so not a member of $\mathfrak I$; the index it abbreviates, $I=\IR$, IS a member** — $\mathfrak I$ admits half-lines and $\IR$ by C3's own definition, and it has to, since $\lvert\Theta_1\rvert\le\bar\Theta$ is read off C3(ii) at $I=\IR$ at four or more sites. Stated in C3(i) so that $\Theta_1$ (digit) and $\Theta_I$ (italic capital) are not read as two values of one index. **Retyped 2026-08-15 (dispatch 27): the row and C3(i) both read "…$I=\IR$ … not a member of $\mathfrak I$", which contradicted C3's "half-lines and $\IR$ included" and would have withdrawn the envelope bound from the interior kernel.** Vanishing off $I$, the **envelope** bound $\lvert\Theta_I\rvert\le\bar\Theta$ and the **truncated** moments `eq:truncmom`, all from C3(ii), uniformly in $h$ and $I$; $\Theta_1$ symmetric and Lipschitz by C3(iii). **Was a convention "(H2c)" in a stale, non-compiling file and was cited at eleven live sites with no declaration in any live file**; defined in C3 since round 12 and pointed at by (P9). **Round-12 support clause $[-c_Cs_0,c_C]$ retired 2026-08-15**: the shipped kernel `k_order(., 8)` is Gaussian-based and compactly supported for no finite $c_C$, so the support clause was **false of the estimator the paper runs**; C3(ii) now posits an envelope | `eq:rescaled`, C3, (P9), `lem:bdryfam` |
| ~~$s_0$~~ → $I$, $\mathfrak I$ | interval in $\mathfrak I$ | **RETIRED 2026-08-15 (dispatch 26). The boundary index is no longer a scalar; it is the realised truncation interval.** $\mathfrak I:=\{I=[a,b]:a\in[-\infty,0],\ b\in[0,+\infty],\ b-a\ge2\}$, C3; $I(x^\ast):=[(\underline x-x^\ast)/h_x,(\bar x-x^\ast)/h_x]$, `eq:ivalx`, and $I_{ij}:=I(X_{ij})$. **What the change buys, and it is not cosmetic:** the interval records *how far* the nearer endpoint is **and** *which* endpoint it is, so `lem:intker`(iv)'s three cases and its reflection convention collapse to one line, the reflection becomes a **theorem** (`lem:bdryfam`(f)), and the **both-endpoints-close** configuration — which no value of a scalar $s_0$ could name, and which round 12 excluded by hypothesis — is covered by the same clause. The retired scalar's own defining equation `eq:s0def` is deleted. ~~**SEPARATION, AND IT IS SWEPT RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** Italic $I$ also names the **identity** operator/matrix (`prp:norangeinv`(i), `assumptions_supp:1053`) and the Roman-numeral term labels (I), (II), (III) of `lem:sieveproj`; the interval never shares a display with any of them, appearing only as a subscript on $\Theta$, as the second argument of $C_h$, and as a domain of integration $\int_I$.~~ **BOTH HALVES OF THAT PARAGRAPH WERE FALSE AND THE SEPARATION IS NOW STRUCTURAL, 2026-08-27 (C111).** *(a) The closure claim is false at the family's own argumented forms*: the interval is also written $I(x^\ast)$, $I_{ij}$, $I^\ast$, $\inf I$, $\sup I$, $\lvert I\rvert$, $I\in\mathfrak I$, $\mathsf G(I)$ and $I\mapsto\Theta_I(\varsigma)$ — nine shapes outside the three the sentence lists, and $I(x^\ast)$ is *grammatically identical* to an operator applied to an argument, which is precisely the failure mode that broke "$\mathcal B$ never carries a subscript" against $\mathcal B(\mathcal X)$. *(b) The non-co-occurrence claim is a defence this file has found unavailable four times, and `results_supp.tex` carried **both** readings — the identity at 6 lines and the interval at ~50 — with two of them inside **statements** (`lem:balance`'s and `lem:bdryfam`'s). **The disposition is therefore a rename and not a rule: the identity map is $\mathrm{Id}$ at all 24 of its compiled lines** (`assumptions_supp.tex:1070,1071`; `results_supp.tex:1717,3489,4291,4308,5061,5398`; `proofs.tex:4271,4274,5016,5017,5085,5151,5155,5210,7257,8645,8648,8734,8738,8739,10009,10017` (line `:8734` carries two occurrences)), declared in compiled text at its first site in compiled order. **Italic $I$ now has one meaning.** Why the *index* did not move instead — five to one on site count, the $I\in\mathfrak I$ pairing, C41's live decline of $\mathsf J$, C80(a) barring a variant Greek capital for a binding symbol, and the digit-$1$ clash with $\Theta_1$ that rules out every font-variant of the letter — is argued at §1.7's letter-$I$ row. *The Roman-numeral term labels (I), (II), (III) are `\text{(I)}` and are not math italic at all, so they were never a competitor and this sentence should not have listed them.* Fraktur $\mathfrak I$ stands against **bare** $\mathcal I$ (the identified set, `def:admissible`) and **subscripted** $\mathcal I_a,\mathcal I_m,\mathcal I_{-k}$ (the sample folds). **Sweep, d26: bare $\mathcal I$ → `results_s2_identification` 6 lines, `proofs` 11 lines (`:2113`–`:2581`); $\mathfrak I$ → `assumptions_main` 6, `assumptions_supp` 1, `results_supp` 11, `proofs` 4 (`:1763`, `:6252`, `:6823`, `:8436`). ZERO SHARED LINES AND ZERO SHARED SECTIONS**, the nearest pair being ~3,700 lines apart — which is the criterion C66 used to *reject* $\mathfrak R$ (four lines from $\mathcal R$) and which is met here. Fraktur is this project's designated third font for a letter with live meanings: $\mathfrak B$ (C20), $\mathfrak m$ (C61), and C61's ground was exactly "$\mathfrak m$ occurs in no file" — $\mathfrak I$ occurred in no file | C3, `eq:ivalx`, `lem:bdryfam` |
| $\bar\Theta$ | $\IR\to[0,\infty)$ | the **kernel envelope** of C3(ii): measurable, bounded, integrable, with $\int\lvert\varsigma\rvert^{p_K}\bar\Theta<\infty$, and dominating $\lvert\Theta_I\rvert$ **uniformly over $I\in\mathfrak I$**. It is what replaced compact support on 2026-08-15, and the two constants every downstream bound reads are *derived from it*: $\mathsf c_2=\lVert\bar\Theta\rVert_\infty$ and $c_C=\lVert\bar\Theta\rVert_{L^1}/(2\mathsf c_2)$, `eq:envconst`, so that $2c_C\mathsf c_2=\lVert\bar\Theta\rVert_{L^1}$. **The bar is the corpus's envelope accent and this is its fourth use**, beside $\bar w(t)$ and $\bar f_k$ (C2$'$(i)'s dominating functions) and $\bar c_\pi$ — it is not a mean and never a closure. **Fixed at $p_K=8$ by `lem:bdryfam`(d) as the *minimal* envelope $\sup_{I\in\mathfrak I}\lvert\Theta_I\rvert$** — continuous (hence measurable) by (d)'s two semicontinuity paragraphs, finite because the explicit majorant $\underline g^{-1}(\sum_k\varsigma^{2k})^{1/2}e^{-\varsigma^2/2}$ dominates it, and *minimal*, so no admissible envelope returns a smaller $(\mathsf c_2,c_C)$. **Measured: $\mathsf c_2=34.0$, $\lVert\bar\Theta\rVert_{L^1}=10.56$, $c_C=0.155$, uniformly over $\mathfrak I$ and attained at $\lvert I\rvert=2$.** *The majorant is not the envelope and its pair $(4.50\times10^8,\,1.82)$ is not quoted as one*: a d27 draft defined $\bar\Theta$ as the majorant, on a magnitude test read off $c_C$ alone; the test is on the product $2c_C\mathsf c_2$, which the majorant misses by eight orders | C3(ii), `eq:envconst`, `lem:bdryfam` |
| $\underline g$ | positive number | $\inf_{I\in\mathfrak I}\lambda_{\min}(\mathsf G(I))$, `lem:bdryfam`(c) — the **uniform floor on the Gram spectrum** that makes the boundary family's coefficients uniformly bounded and hence $\bar\Theta$ finite. **Measured on `eq:gramfam` as printed (bare $e^{-\varsigma^2/2}$): $6.59\times10^{-8}$, attained at $I=[-2,0]$; $2.718\times10^{-1}$ at $I=\IR$; $1.51\times10^{-5}$ over half-lines — and $1.51\times10^{-5}$, not the $2.57\times10^{-1}$ an earlier draft quoted, is also the infimum over the intervals the application realises**, attained exactly at every bandwidth it runs, a boundary design point realising a truncated half-line and $\mathsf G([0,\infty])-\mathsf G([0,L])\succeq0$ forcing the inequality. *Not a rate constant, and **no consumed constant** is computed from it*: $\underline g$ reaches every bound below only through the finiteness — and, by `lem:bdryfam`(g), the **decay** — it certifies for $\bar\Theta$, never through $\mathsf c_2$, which is read off $\bar\Theta$ directly. **It is a printed constant, at three places, and this row said the opposite until 2026-08-20 — one row above the row that prints the pair.** `eq:envconst` evaluated at the explicit majorant returns $(4.50\times10^8,\,1.82)$, printed in C3's note at `assumptions_main.tex:3599–3601`; *of that pair only the **first** entry is computed from $\underline g$*, since $\underline g^{-1}$ cancels in the ratio defining $c_C$. `lem:bdryfam`'s own note prints a third value, $1.97\times10^6$ — the same sup-norm formed at the realised family's infimum $1.51\times10^{-5}$. **All three are comparison values and enter no bound**; the comparison is what makes the minimal envelope a ruling with a price rather than a preference. **The underline is the corpus's lower-bound accent**, as in $\underline x$ (the left endpoint of $\mathcal X$) and $\underline\lambda_n$; it is separated from the busy italic $g$ — $\hat g_i$, `lem:nusigma`'s test function, $\nu_g/\sigma_g$ — by being a **scalar constant** where every $g$ is a function, and it carries no argument, ever | `lem:bdryfam`(c)–(d) |
| $\mathsf G(I)$ | $p_K\times p_K$ positive-definite matrix | the **Gram matrix of the monomials against the Gaussian weight on $I$**, `eq:gramfam`: $\mathsf G(I)_{jk}=\int_I\varsigma^{j+k}e^{-\varsigma^2/2}d\varsigma$. Bound in `lem:bdryfam`'s statement, dies in its proof, consumed by no other result. **Sans-serif against `lem:intker`(iii)'s italic $G$** — the bounded-variation function of that clause, a §1.6 local bound by its own hypothesis — and the two never share a display. *Admitted with a row rather than renamed*, on the C95/C97/C101 ground the four-font $B$ family was admitted on; the alternatives were swept and all taken ($\mathcal G$ = `lem:density`'s closed span, $\mathfrak G$/$\mathbf G$ a further font of the same letter, Greek capitals all live). **The Gaussian weight itself carries no symbol** and is written out as $e^{-\varsigma^2/2}$: $\phi$ is already live in two §1.6-local readings (the standard normal density in the §2 counterexample; a generic convex/test function in `lem:condjensen` and `prp:bierensid`), and promoting a local to a binding symbol is the C25 defect. **Both citations in this row repointed from `eq:gram` to `eq:gramfam` on 2026-08-15 (dispatch 27): `eq:gram` is `prp:tccrepair`'s sample Gram $\mathbf G_n$ (row above), and the boundary family's Gram was renamed to `eq:gramfam` the same day the collision was found — this row was the last live trace of it** | `eq:gramfam`, `lem:bdryfam` |
| $c_\Theta(\epsilon)$ | positive number | the **envelope tail radius**: the least $c$ with $\int_{\lvert\varsigma\rvert>c}\bar\Theta\le\epsilon$, `lem:intker`(i). Joins $c_C,c_\Phi,c_\Pi$ in the constant family and is subscripted by the object whose tail it truncates. **Never written without its argument** — the separator §1.7 already uses for $\lambda(u,t)$ and $\mathcal V(\varphi_\circ,\rho)$. Consumed at exactly two sites, both of them repairs of a compact-support step: `prp:tccfails`'s hypothesis and Step 3 ($h_u<1/(2c_\Theta(\epsilon))$ in place of $h_u<1/(2c_C)$), and `eq:thetasqlb` at $\epsilon=\tfrac12$. **THE NAME IS A SUBSTITUTION AND THE REASON MATTERS:** the natural $M_\epsilon$ was **unavailable in either font** — bare italic $M$ is retired by C7, and sans-serif $\mathsf M_\epsilon$ is *already live* as `prp:stopfeas`'s $O_P$ constant at six occurrences (C99/C100). Writing $M_\epsilon$ would have opened a new collision on the very letter this dispatch also repaired at `prp:orthog`(iii) | `lem:intker`(i), `eq:thetasqlb` |
| $\omega_n$ / $\omega_\ast$ / $\omega$ | positive scalars; sample point | $\omega_n$ is the **normalising factor** `eq:normconst`, $\asymp h_u^{-1}h_x^{-1/2}$, deterministic and divergent, equivalently the Landweber relaxation parameter $\omega_n^{-2}$; $\omega_\ast$ is `lem:scaleinv`'s **generic** scale; **bare $\omega$ is the sample point** of the probability space, at (P6), (P7a), `lem:adjfubini`, `prp:compact` and `lem:adjoint`(iii). C67 claimed bare $\omega$ occurred in no file; that was false at 55 sites, and the correction is C71 | `eq:normconst`, `lem:scaleinv`, C67, C71 |
